Budget: **34** tasks. Known rows **30**, reserve **4**, one per named risk that can add rows —
D-035's answer, D-010's answer, the GitHub-mirror prerequisite, and the accessibility-statement
ruling. Crossing **34** costs a rider signed by [andres] naming what it displaces (D-031).

stops being one.** T-1207, T-1208 and T-1209 take the count from **30 to 33**, and they are spent
on **none of the four named risks**: they answer **D-040**, which did not exist when this budget
was written. The front page shipped SQL that summed a text column on every database Drupal
supports, and no gate caught it.

**One reserve row remains, and two named risks are still open** — the GitHub-mirror prerequisite
(T-804, blocked on [andres] creating the repository) and the accessibility-statement ruling. If
both need a row, this unit crosses 34 and needs the D-031 rider. **That is a foreseeable overrun,
not a surprise, and it is written here while it is still cheap to plan for.** D-035 and D-010 were
both answered without consuming a row, which is why the reserve reached this point intact.

**D-044's own test, applied term by term, because "necessary" is the word that entire ruling turns on.** By its wording — *work without which something already signed is broken, false, or impossible to ship* — **three** of the twelve qualify and **nine do not**, and saying so is the point of this paragraph:

- **Necessary — T-1306 and T-1308**, the currency unit on the two front-page figures and on the seven register field instances. A transparency portal whose headline number is `592,470.00` with no unit anywhere in its HTML is ambiguous about the one thing it exists to publish — and the package is not even silent, it is **inconsistent**: six shipped PDFs already print `21300.00 EUR` beside a register table rendering `21,300.00` for the same person (D-047). That is *false*, not merely *improvable*.
- **Necessary — T-1305**, the cumulative award chart. **T-1103 is signed scope that is currently undone**, and its success criterion is already the chart's acceptance criterion verbatim. T-1305 discharges it; it does not enlarge it.
- **Useful, not necessary — the other nine.** T-1301 (radius), T-1302 (hero primary action), T-1303 (tile icons), T-1304 (watermark), T-1307 (the seventh axe page), T-1309 (header navigation), T-1310 (`block_6` moves), T-1311 (the legal bottom bar) and T-1312 (the legal destinations). Nothing signed is broken or false without them. ⚠️ **T-1311 and T-1312 are the closest to the line** and still fall on this side of it: a public-sector footer with no privacy or accessibility link is a real gap, but it is a gap T-1215 named in writing and shipped deliberately rather than an untruth.

**What the growth rests on is a person, not an argument: [andres] approved the design round on 2026-09-04**, and nine of these twelve rows are that approval turned into work. That is a legitimate basis — he is the one who signs scope — and it is **not** the basis D-044 describes. The two are written down separately here so nobody later reads twelve rows of design work as though the necessity test had been passed twelve times.

Wave 15 rested on **measured failures** — two red pipelines. **These two rest on a defect found by opening the page**, which is neither: no pipeline went red, no gate went amber, and no decision schedules them. The sign-in work's own documentation states the defect — core's form dropped into a 75rem column, a 60-character input running most of the way across a desktop window, and **no route at all to the password reset**, because this theme places no local-task block and the link had therefore disappeared rather than moved.

⚠️ **And the gate that could have caught it was already looking straight at it.** `/user/login` is the **seventh** entry in the theme's axe `PAGES` array — committed, unmodified, carrying `expect: 'form#user-login-form'` — so the blocking `nightwatch` job has been scanning that page and reporting no violations. **It was right to.** An ugly page with zero violations is an accessible ugly page, and no gate this project owns has an opinion about a 60-character input in a 75rem column. That is the honest reason this basis is a fourth category rather than a variant of wave 15's.

**Both rows are `H`: they land in the sibling theme checkout, not here.** ⚠️ **T-1602 is NOT a first instalment of D-051's tenant 4.** The *writing* currency editor needs a module that does not exist and has not been approved — D-051's deferral is signed, its module is an **open ruling** — while this panel writes nothing. Shipping it commits the project to nothing, and that is the property that lets it ship from a theme at all.

## Wave 9 · The mechanisms are proven before a single node is authored

Nothing lands in `content/` in this wave. Every row below exists because a mechanism this unit
depends on **fails silently**, and a silent failure produces a green gate over an empty result.
Every probe must be **run**, not reasoned about — I-066, recorded today.

| # | Repo | Task | Success criterion (falsifiable) | Blocked by |
|---|---|---|---|---|

Baseline denominators for T-1001: **92 enabled modules · 516 config objects**, snapshot `t901-baseline` retained. 🔴 **`composer show drupal/agora_theme` → `1.0.0`, from `ftp.drupal.org`, and the installed `templates/` holds 7 files with NO `views-view-table.html.twig`** (the local checkout holds 8; `git tag --contains dbbe934` → **0**). Every Views table on this rig renders through core's stock template. 🔴 **A hazard CLAUDE.md declares closed was open**: `~/agora-smoke/source` still carried a remote named `origin` with a **live push URL**, so a bare `git push` there would have reached the canonical repository. Renamed to `drupalcode`, push disabled, falsified — `git push` now exits **128**, `git fetch` still works. Rebuild, never pull — a rig that was pulled is not a clean-install surface | `diff` of the rig's `require` against the template's `composer.json` `require` produces **0 lines**. `drush status` reports the site installed. **`composer show drupal/agora_theme` prints the resolved version and it is quoted in the row's evidence**; if it is `1.0.0` the row records, in writing, that `templates/views-view-table.html.twig` does not exist in it (I-063) and that every table rendered on this rig is therefore pre-fix. | T-902 ✓ | T | ⚠️ **Its subject was cancelled and the row is NOT withdrawn (rule 8): D-035=C means no content will ship a `translations:` key, so this probe now stops a thing being built rather than enabling one — which is a legitimate outcome for a probe, and the cheaper of the two.** Its finding stays live in `IDIOMS.md` for whoever adds `translations:` later: the importer drops a translation for a language the site has not configured **silently, with no log line at all**. ✅ **Signed 2026-08-26, and it found a 🔴 that would have broken T-1001.** Both outcomes quoted on **fresh databases proven distinct by `system.site:uuid`** — six applies, six different UUIDs, so no run was a re-apply hiding behind `Existing::Skip`. Clean: `translation languages: [en, es]`, `hasTranslation(es): TRUE`. Applied on a clean rig. ⚠️ Nothing from this probe is committed to `content/`; it proves the mechanism, it does not ship | **Both** outcomes quoted. ---

## Wave 10 · The corpus — two lanes, disjoint by repository

**Lane A (`T`)** touches only `config/`, `content/`, `recipe.yml`.
**Lane B (`H`)** touches only `agora-theme/`: `templates/`, `css/`, a new `config/optional/`,
`tests/src/Nightwatch/`. Zero shared files.

⚠️ **Lane A is internally SEQUENTIAL despite its disjoint files**, because every row passes through
**one export rig**, and a rig is a single mutable resource: two modelling sessions produce a
baseline diff that belongs to neither. Disjoint files are not disjoint work when the tool is shared.

⚠️ **The `tester` starts in parallel with both lanes and its early failures are expected.** An
assertion that the library view has 26 rows is *supposed* to be red until the 26th node exists. A
red wave-10 morning is the plan working, not a regression.

A rule that did not run cannot have passed, and axe files an unapplied rule in a bucket that reads exactly like a pass (I-045). Original text follows. **Pager heading level** — carried debt: *"core defaults `#pagination_heading_level` to `h4` and `heading-order` fired during development. | T-1011 ✓ | T | ✅ **Signed 2026-08-26, and it MOVED REPOSITORY on evidence — the row is now `T`, not `H`.** The implementer sent to the theme **stopped without writing code**, and found something one rung earlier than the boundary argument it was given: ⚠️ **the three fragments never pass through this theme at all.** They render only on **admin routes**, and admin routes are rendered by **Gin** — measured: `system.theme` default `agora_theme`, admin `gin`, `node.settings use_admin_theme: true`, and on `/node/add`, `/node/add/agora_base_contract` and `/admin/structure/types` the gin assets count **1** and the agora_theme assets count **0**, while five front-end pages carry **0 fragments** between them. A `hook_preprocess` fires only when the theme is active; on those routes it is not, and that is true of **every** install because the recipe sets only `system.theme.default` while `drupal_cms_admin_ui` supplies Gin. **So the marking belongs where the sentence is authored**, and it is a mechanism about language rather than about phrases: `<span lang="es">` travels with the words, so a reword carries it instead of orphaning it, and no repository learns anything about another. **Three one-line edits** to the shipped descriptions. Verified end to end on a live install rather than inferred: `/node/add` **2** spans, `/node/add/agora_base_contract` **1**, `/admin/structure/types` **2**, rendering as `known in Spanish law as the <span lang="es">convenio</span>`. 🟡 **The axe half of the row could NOT be honoured and refusing it is the finding.** `valid-lang`'s scope is elements with `lang` **other than `<html>`**, and the theme emits **0** of them — on `/contracts` the 29 apparent hits are `hreflang` on taxonomy links and the true count of `lang=` is **1**, on `<html>`. Naming it would have forced that assertion to accept `inapplicable` — **the I-045 false green welded into the gate**, in the row that warns about it. That is **SC 3.1.2 Language of Parts (AA)**, live now and owed under D-033 regardless of how D-035 was answered, and this was the only row that would have caught it. **New shape:** those three fragments carry `lang="es"`; the count of annotated fragments is printed; `html-has-lang` and `valid-lang` are **named in the axe rules-run list** and report 0 violations. ⚠️ axe checks that a `lang` value is *valid*, **not** that it is *correct* — so the count and a named spot-check are the evidence and the axe green is a floor. **No longer gated on D-035**; it is signed. Original text follows. **`lang` attributes on the English parts of a Spanish page** — **WCAG 2.2 SC 3.1.2 Language of Parts (AA)**. Config-sourced strings are English by D-033 and the Spanish translation cannot reach an installed site (D-033's own ⚠️, four locks). A Spanish page therefore carries English field labels, view titles, facet headings and pager text, and **unmarked they are a 3.1.2 failure**. **Notes on wave 12's rows, recorded here rather than by editing them (rule 8). [ejecutor] 2026-09-20.**

- 🔴 **T-1203's premise is HALF FALSE ON DISK, and the true half is a worse defect than the row describes.** The row says D-032 step 7's fourth pre-commit check *"is **replaced by name**, in the commit that first makes it false"*, and the reconciliation pass below says *"Nothing in `tests/bin/` replaces"* it. **Both were checked against the disk before any work started, and the retirement had already happened.** `specs/000-project/DECISIONS.md` carries `⚠️ **RETIRED 2026-08-26, in the commit that first made it false - by name, never deleted.**` inside D-032's own amendment, and it names `tests/bin/media-licence` as the replacement. **Measured rather than believed**: that paragraph landed in **`17ac5fb`**, and `17ac5fb` is **the same commit** that took `content/` from **1** file to **3** by shipping `MEDIA-LICENCES.md` and `PEOPLE.md`. The decision did exactly what it claims to have done.
- 🔴 **What was genuinely undone is the copy people follow, and it stood for 24 days.** `.claude/skills/exportar-config-limpia/SKILL.md` carried `find content -type f | wc -l  # must not have grown` **verbatim and unmarked**, inside the five-line block a reader copies and pastes before committing an export. So the *decision* was amended and the *procedure* was not. **That is the project's standing failure mode one level up from a stale number: a rule written in two places goes stale in the copy nobody has to open** - the same shape as the `451 - 41 = 410` arithmetic that sat in `CLAUDE.md` beside a `455/414` bullet, found and fixed in the same commit as this. Retired there now, by name, with the replacement named in the line that replaces it.
- ⚠️ **The replacement's GAP, stated so that "retired" is not read as "covered".** `media-licence` enumerates `content/` in both scopes, is FATAL if either enumeration is zero - which is what the `1` was really guarding, that the enumeration happened at all - and checks every shipped binary against `content/MEDIA-LICENCES.md` in **both directions**. A stray **entity export**, a `.yml` under `content/<entity_type>/`, has no manifest row to be missing and would pass. `ValidationTest`'s published-record count and `generate-demo-media.py`'s per-directory enumeration would notice; **neither is a pre-commit check, and that gap has no owner.**
- **The invariant sweep T-1203 asks for. Every denominator, measured 2026-09-20 on commit `a358cd7`, findings 0 everywhere.** Seventeen invariants across both runners, which is the total `CLAUDE.md` states.

**Approved in substance by [andres] on 2026-09-04**, as a design round rather than as a ruling on any
of its parts. **Two** of the twelve rows are blocked on a record that is **PREPARED and UNSIGNED** —

**D-047**, is also prepared and unsigned, but T-1306 and T-1308 are **not** blocked on it: it records
a direction [andres] gave in words on 2026-09-04 rather than a question waiting on him. The budget arithmetic
for all twelve is the sixth reserve-accounting entry at the top of this file; nine of them are
**useful rather than necessary** by D-044's own test, and that is stated there rather than implied here.

> **Legend addition for this wave only — `T + H`.** One row, **T-1305**, spans **both** repositories. A commit
> cannot span two repositories, and two green pipelines do not prove the two halves agree, because
> each pipeline only ever sees its own side. Every `T + H` row therefore takes its evidence from a
> **rendered page on the rig**, not from a pair of job lists.

**Notes on existing rows, recorded here rather than by editing them (rule 8).**

- **T-1313 and T-1314 take wave 13 from twelve rows to FOURTEEN, and neither was in the approved twelve.** They are appended rather than substituted: T-1313 closes a window the theme lane opened the same day (the theme deleted the photograph from its own package, so the demonstration had to start shipping it), and T-1314 is the discharge of a dated exemption that came due. Both are recorded against the wave's reserve accounting rather than hidden inside another row.
- **T-1103 is DISCHARGED by T-1305, not duplicated by it.** Its success criterion is already the chart's acceptance criterion word for word. It does not become a second row, and this wave's twelve do not include it.
- 🔴 **A FILE OUTSIDE THESE FOUR ROWS WAS EDITED, and it is reported here rather than buried in a diff: `config/views.view.agora_base_publications.yml`.** Publishing the first non-`agora_base_*` node this package has ever shipped uncovered a defect in the register that no corpus could previously show. The `type` filter on the **`default`** display is `exposed: true`, and an exposed bundle filter with nothing submitted **applies no restriction at all** - its `value` list is the set of options OFFERED, not a constraint. **Said in one sentence: the Type dropdown offers exactly six options and no more - `reduce: true` sees to that, and `Utility page` was never offered - but its `- Any -` meant _any node type on the site_ rather than _any of the six above it_.** The guard makes the default mean what the control implies. Until 2026-09-05 the site published nothing but the six bundles, so the laxness was invisible; the moment four `page` nodes were published they appeared **in `/publications` as rows labelled `Utility page`** with an empty area cell, and - because `block_1` overrides only `pager` and inherits the default filters - **on the front page as well: `grep -c "Utility page"` on `/` returned 4.** Measured, then falsified in both directions: `/publications` rendered **60 rows against 56 published records**, and `ValidationTest::testBaseSurfacesAndMenu` **failed 16-versus-12 on the last page with the four pages present and passed `OK (1 test, 169 assertions)` with them removed**, on otherwise identical config and test. ⚠️ **Exactly one display of one view is affected**: all seven other views and all five `block_*` overrides already carry `exposed: false`, checked one by one. **The fix is a second, non-exposed `type_1` bundle filter on the `default` display**, pinning the six registers, so the exposed filter still works as the Type facet but can only narrow WITHIN them - the standard Views pattern, and the shape `block_2` already uses. After it: front page `Utility page` **4 -> 0**, `/publications` **60 -> 56 rows**, the four legal pages still **200**, the two front-page tables and their captions unchanged and D-041's frame sentence still present. **This is a pre-existing defect in signed config, surfaced rather than caused - but it ships broken without the fix, so the fix rides with the change that reveals it.** It needs the orquestador's eye: it touches a view T-1310 also plans to touch.
- **T-1105 is a PREREQUISITE of T-1311, not discharged by it.** The legal bar *links* the accessibility statement; writing the statement is still T-1105's work, still undone, and still one of the four named risks the reserve accounting has tracked since its first entry. T-1311 cannot go green without it.
- **T-1106 (`screenshot.webp`) must run AFTER this wave, or the row is wasted.** Its criterion is a screenshot of a rendered demo page carrying real content; taken before these twelve rows it photographs a front page that no longer exists by the time anyone installs the template, and the placeholder would have been replaced by something equally stale.
- **T-1204 must RE-RUN, because every denominator this wave touches moves.** axe pages 6 → 7; the theme's assertion total, last read at 362; `contrast-check`'s token and pair counts; `no-varchar-aggregate`'s displays scanned; the spellcheck denominator; and PHPUnit's assertion count in both database jobs. T-1204's own signed text is the reason: *"a table nobody re-opened is a claim, not a measurement."*
- ✅ **T-1313's withheld recipe action is DISCHARGED and LANDED, 2026-09-06 — and the discharge cost a second file, which is the part worth reading.** T-1313 calls the withholding *"the row's most important sentence"*: `agora_theme.settings` existed in no published theme release, so `recipe.yml` carried the three lines as a comment and `ValidationTest` **asserted the withholding**, watched failing with the action in place. [andres] published **`agora_theme` 1.1.0** — `<date>` epoch **1788646865**, which is **2026-09-05 22:21 UTC** and 00:21 on the **6th** at this repository's own +0200, so both dates are right and neither is loose — `project_status: published`, `<security covered="1">`, `core_compatibility: ^11` — and the tarball on ftp.drupal.org carries `config/install/agora_theme.settings.yml` (`hero_image_path: ''`) and `config/schema/agora_theme.schema.yml`, **read out of the archive rather than trusted from the theme's working copy**. The action is now live in `recipe.yml` and the `ValidationTest` assertion is inverted in the same commit. ✅ **And the flip is falsified here rather than inherited from T-1313's record.** The old assertion was restored on top of the landed action on `~/agora-hero` and the single test run: **1 failure**, *"Failed asserting that an array contains 'public://hero-wide.webp'"*, `Tests: 1, Assertions: 10, Failures: 1`, exit **1**. Shipped file restored, same test, same rig: **`OK (1 test, 13 assertions)`** — the three extra assertions being exactly the ones the failure had stopped short of. The probe left nothing behind: the rig's copy is byte-identical to the working copy again, checked with `diff`. ⚠️ **`composer.json` moved from `^1.0` to `^1.1` in that same commit and THAT is the load-bearing half**: at `^1.0` a resolver may still legitimately install **1.0.7**, which carries no settings object, and the action would then throw on exactly the installs the withholding existed to protect. ✅ **THAT SENTENCE WAS REASONED FROM CORE SOURCE AND NEVER MEASURED, SO IT IS MEASURED HERE, AND IT IS THE MOST USEFUL THING IN THIS NOTE.** On the same rig, with the landed action in `recipe.yml`, the package's own constraint was put back to `^1.0`: Composer answered *"Downgrading drupal/agora_theme (1.1.0 => 1.0.7)"* from `.../agora_theme-1.0.7.zip` — so a resolver really does pick it, this is not a hypothetical — and **1.0.7 has no `config/install/` directory at all** (it does still ship `images/hero-wide.webp` of its own, which is why nothing regressed while the action was withheld). A clean `site:install minimal` plus `drush recipe` then exited **1** with `In SimpleConfigUpdate.php line 52: Config agora_theme.settings does not exist so can not be updated`. The constraint is load-bearing as a fact, not as an argument. ✅ **And the other direction on the same rig, which is what separates "the version caused it" from "something caused it":** the constraint restored to `^1.1`, the theme re-locked at **1.1.0** from `.../agora_theme-1.1.0.zip` **with no root requirement naming it at all** — `Locking drupal/agora_theme (1.1.0)` follows from `drupal/agora_transparency (1.x-dev e8e18a3)` and from nothing else — clean install, apply exit **0**, `83/83`, and `hero_image_path` back to `public://hero-wide.webp` with the front page emitting the custom property **once** and serving the file at **200 / 90,746 bytes / sha256 `7eaa0b36…`**. ⚠️ **T-1313 names "1.0.8" as the newest published release and that is FALSE** — neither 1.0.4 nor 1.0.8 appears in `updates.drupal.org/release-history/agora_theme/current` or in Composer's facade; the published 1.0.x list is 1.0.0-1.0.3 and 1.0.5-1.0.7. The row is not edited (rule 8); the correction is here, and it changes nothing about its argument, because no 1.0.x release ships the object. **`composer.json:7` is the only version constraint on the theme in the whole package** — `recommended.yml` is still an empty curated list and names no version of anything, checked by grep across every tracked `*.json`, `*.yml` and `*.md`. ✅ **Measured on a rig built from ZERO for this, `~/agora-hero`, because a rig serving a path repository or a hand-copied theme proves nothing here:** fresh `drupal/cms` **2.1.3**, this package added as a Composer path repository, everything else resolved normally. `composer.lock` records `drupal/agora_theme` **1.1.0**, `dist: zip https://ftp.drupal.org/files/projects/agora_theme-1.1.0.zip` — **from packages.drupal.org**, and the installed `agora_theme.info.yml` reads `version: '1.1.0'`, `datestamp: 1788646868`. The installed theme ships **no photograph**: `logo.png` and `screenshot.png` and nothing else. Recipe applied **83/83**, `[OK] Ágora Transparency applied successfully`, on a `minimal` install of Drupal **11.4.6**, `Default theme: agora_theme`, **61 nodes / 60 published**. `agora_theme.settings:hero_image_path` reads **`public://hero-wide.webp`** (the stored form); `file_managed` fid **43** at that uri, `filesize 90746`, `status 1`. ✅ **Rendered at 1440×900 in Chrome:** `style='--agora-hero-image: url("/sites/default/files/hero-wide.webp");'` on `#block-agora-theme-page-title`, emitted **once**; `::before` computed `background-image` resolving to that file at `opacity 0.42`; **one** network request for it, HTTP **200**, **90,746 bytes**, sha256 `7eaa0b36…` identical to the packaged copy; `<h1>` still **1**. ✅ **Control on the same rig, which is what makes the render attributable to the action rather than to anything else:** with the setting emptied the custom property is emitted **0** times, `::before` computes `background-image: none`, and there are **zero** requests for the file. ⚠️ **T-1313's evidence says `::before` and does not say ON WHAT, and there are two candidates one line apart.** The picture is on **`#block-agora-theme-page-title::before`** (`opacity 0.42`, which is the figure T-1313 records, so the row was right); **`.agora-hero::before` is a different pseudo-element** carrying the mark's radial tint at `opacity 0.58`. The first probe written for this measurement asked the second one and got `radial-gradient(416px 320px at calc(100% - 320px) 50%, rgb(13, 43, 77) …)` — a real computed value, truthfully reported, about the wrong element. Named here because the failure produces evidence rather than an error, which is the kind that gets written into a row.
- **T-1310: the mechanism was decided by a measurement, and the row's line number had drifted.** [ejecutor] 2026-09-20. ⚠️ **The row cites D-041's frame sentence at `config/views.view.agora_base_publications.yml:413`; on disk it is at `:431`**, moved by the `type_1` guard the note four bullets above records. The sentence is byte-identical and the reconciliation pass already records `:431` against T-1210, so this is drift in the row rather than a change to the sentence - and the new test **reads the sentence out of `block_2`'s `header` area instead of quoting it**, so no third copy of it exists to go stale. **The cards are placed as a BLOCK, not as an attachment display and not as a views area**, and three things decided that rather than taste. (1) The row's own criterion says *"`block_6` renders on `/publications`"* - converting the display to an attachment renames it and makes the criterion's own words false. (2) **The heading belongs to the PLACEMENT, not to the view**: `Every record, by service area` came from the Canvas component's `views_label`, and an attachment or a views area would have rendered six cards under no heading at all - a real accessibility regression bought for a tidier diff. (3) **The region and the weight are measured, not guessed.** `agora_theme` 1.1.0 declares exactly three regions - `header`, `content`, `footer` - so `content` is the only body region that exists, and its four theme-side blocks in it are weighted `messages -30`, `breadcrumb -25`, `page_title -20`, `content 0`; **`weight: -10`** therefore lands the cards under the `<h1>` and above the register. Read from the published release on `git.drupalcode.org`, which is what a clean install resolves (`Locking drupal/agora_theme (1.1.0)`) - **not** from the sibling checkout, which another implementer holds open today.
- ⚠️ **T-1310 has a consequence the row does not mention, applied and named here rather than left in the diff.** `block_6` carried `use_more: true` with `link_display: page_1` and the text *"Browse every record by service area"*. On the landing page that was a link to the register; **on the register it is a link to the page you are already on**, which is the one thing a "more" link must never be. `use_more` is now `false`; `use_more_text` and `link_display` are left in place, inert, because that is the state the Views UI stores when the toggle goes off. The link was measured rendering on the rig before the change, so this removes something real rather than something hypothetical.
- ⚠️ **What T-1310 could NOT verify in this session, stated rather than glossed.** The rendered result. The DDEV rig serves a PREVIOUS install and this session was forbidden from rebuilding it, so the rig gives the BEFORE state only - and it gives it exactly: `/` (reached through `/home` → 301) prints `agora-area-cards` **1**, `Every record, by service area` **1**, `fictional Spanish municipality` **1** and **6** rendered `.views-row`, while `/publications` prints `agora-area-cards` **0**. Those are the four counts this change inverts or preserves. The AFTER state is asserted by `ValidationTest::testServiceAreaCardsMoveToTheRegister()` on a clean install in the pipeline, which is where *"asserted in the rendered markup"* is actually discharged - **and it is the pipeline, not this note, that discharges it.**
- 🔴 **T-1310's first push turned the gate RED, the red was REAL, and the shape said so before anything was retried.** Pipeline **`969078`**, commit **`7836bc3`**: eight jobs `success`, **`phpunit` AND `phpunit-pgsql` both `failed`**, `allow_failure=false` on all ten. ⚠️ **That is NOT the MySQL-gone-away shape**, which reds one database and leaves the other green and for which a retry is the right move; **both** databases failing on **the same assertion** is this package's own defect, and a retry would have bought an hour and no information. Read from the mirror's public log rather than guessed, because drupalcode's `/trace` answers **401** anonymously: `Tests: 20, Assertions: 2333, Failures: 1, Deprecations: 137`, and the one failure is `✘ Base surfaces and menu`. ✅ **The new test itself PASSED** - so `block_6` rendering on `/publications`, its absence from the front page, the six rows, the heading and D-041's frame sentence were all discharged on a clean install in that very pipeline.
- 🔴 **The cause is worth more than the fix, and it was latent long before this row.** `VIEW_CONTAINER` - `div[class*="js-view-dom-id-"]` - means *"the view on this page"* only while a page carries exactly ONE view, and every register page did until this row placed a second one beside the register. Mink's text assertions take the **FIRST** match, and at `weight: -10` the cards are first, so `assertEmptyState()` read the cards and reported `ElementTextException: The text "Nothing has been published yet." was not found`. **The selector was never wrong; it was unambiguous by accident, and nothing in the file said so.** Fixed by making it say what it means: a new `PAGE_VIEW` constant anchors it to `#block-agora-theme-content`, on the statement that *the view which IS the page renders inside the main content block and a view this template PLACES beside the page does not* - `recipe.yml` imports that block by name, so the id is on every clean install. And `assertEmptyState` now asserts **exactly one** such container instead of *"at least one"*, so the next second view on a register page fails loudly rather than silently taking the wrong first match. ⚠️ **The other twelve uses of `VIEW_CONTAINER` were checked one by one and are unaffected**: every one of them is scoped to a `<table>`, a form control or a caption, and the cards block renders none of those - which is also why exactly one assertion went red rather than a dozen.
- 🔴 **THE FIX'S SECOND HALF WAS WRONG, AND THE GATE SAID SO ON THE NEXT PUSH - corrected here rather than by rewriting the bullet above (rule 8).** That bullet claims `assertEmptyState` now asserts **exactly one** page-view container *"so the next second view fails loudly"*. Pipeline **`969085`**, commit **`c2765a7`**, falsified it in one line: `✘ Table views` - `ExpectationException: 2 elements matching css "#block-agora-theme-content div[class*=js-view-dom-id-]" found on the page, but should be 1`. **`agora_base_contracts` carries an `attachment_1` display**, and a Views attachment renders a second container **NESTED INSIDE** the page's own one. Two containers there is **correct by design**, so *"exactly one"* was false of a page nobody had broken - a guard that reds on a healthy surface, which I-020 says is worse than no guard at all. ✅ **The scoping half WORKED**: `✘ Base surfaces and menu` went green in the same run, which is the assertion the first red was about.
- ⚠️ **What the corrected constant guarantees, stated exactly, because "scope" and "count" are not the same promise.** A block this template PLACES is always a **sibling** of the main content block and can never be inside it; an attachment can only ever be **inside** the page's own view. So the first match under `PAGE_VIEW` is the page's own view in both cases, and its text subtree contains the attachment's anyway. The constant **scopes and does not count**, and its docblock says so with the pipeline id that proved it. `Tests: 20, Assertions: 2361, Failures: 1` on the mirror for `c2765a7`, against `20 / 2333 / 1` for `7836bc3` - **a different single failure each time, neither of them the same assertion, and neither of them the new test**, which passed in both.
- ✅ **GREEN on the third push, and the verdict is the job list rather than the badge.** Pipeline **`969089`**, ref `1.x`, commit **`def1173`**, read from `/api/v4/projects/project%2Fagora_transparency/pipelines/969089/jobs`: **10 jobs, every one `success`, every one `allow_failure: false`**, `declared: 10 - matched: 10`. The mirror agrees and its **red streak reset to 0** - run `35509324736`, `success`, after `failure x2`. Mirror totals across the three pushes, quoted together because a single figure hides what moved: **`20 / 2333 / 1 failure`** (`7836bc3`), **`20 / 2361 / 1 failure`** (`c2765a7`), **`20 / 2368 / 0`** (`def1173`) - one test added to the mirror's 19, the assertion total rising by **35** across the wave, and **two different single failures neither of which was the new test**. ⚠️ **The drupalcode figure is NOT the same number and is deliberately not quoted here**: those jobs also run `AccessibilityTest`, which the mirror excludes by name, so the two denominators are about different suites and pairing them would be the drift this file keeps warning about.

## Wave 14 · The minimal layout kit

**Scheduled by D-049, signed by [andres] on 2026-09-05** — asked whether to build a layout kit and when, he answered <!-- cspell:disable -->*"Sí, al cerrar esta ronda"*<!-- cspell:enable --> ("yes, on closing this round"). **No row here is blocked on a signature**; D-049 is signed and these six are its second ruling turned into work. The budget arithmetic is the seventh reserve-accounting entry at the top of this file: **53 → 59**, and by D-044's own test **none of the six is *necessary*** — they rest on his signature, which is stated there rather than implied here.

**What this wave is, in one sentence:** Ágora exposes **0** SDC components while `haven_theme` exposes 25 and `byte_theme` 24, so a site owner can rearrange eight blocks and **cannot add a heading or a paragraph** — and four generic components (`section`, `group`, `heading`, `text`) are **57.2%** of everything `haven` places and **55.7%** of everything `byte` places. Four, not twenty-five.

⚠️ **What this wave is NOT, named first because it is one short step away and it is destructive.** It does **not** enable Canvas's page regions. Adding SDC components to the palette and ticking `use_canvas` on the theme settings form are different things, and the second one **silently destroys the footer design** — 2 regions generated, front page still `200` with its 8 `<nav>` landmarks intact, and the theme's own footer classes going from **54 occurrences to 0** (D-049; **I-112**). No row below touches that checkbox, and a row that starts to is out of scope until its own decision exists.

> **The `T + H` legend from wave 13 applies again.** One row, **T-1406**, spans both repositories. A commit cannot span two repositories and two green pipelines do not prove the two halves agree, because each pipeline only ever sees its own side — so its evidence is a **rendered page on the rig**, not a pair of job lists.

| # | Repo | Task | Success criterion (falsifiable) | Blocked by |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| T-1401 ✓ | H | **`heading` and `text` — the two content-bearing components, and the smaller half of the kit.** `components/heading/` and `components/text/` in `agora_theme`, each with its `*.component.yml` and its Twig, drawn from the theme's existing type scale and colour tokens. **No new colour token and no new `@pair`**: a layout kit that introduces a palette is a redesign wearing a different name | **The theme's SDC count goes `0` → `2`**, counted as directories under `components/` holding a `*.component.yml`, and the number printed rather than asserted in prose. Both appear **in the Canvas palette** on a rig after a cache rebuild, quoted from what the palette shows. ⚠️ **The palette observation is the evidence and the file's existence is not**: a component whose metadata Canvas rejects produces **no error** — `ComponentMetadataRequirementsChecker` simply leaves it out, so "the YAML is on disk" is exactly the green that proves nothing. `tests/bin/contrast-check` prints the **same token count and the same pair count** as before this row | — |
| T-1402 ✓ | H | **`section` and `group` — the two layout components, and the only two with slots.** `section` is the band; `group` is the box inside it. Between them they are what lets a site owner put two things side by side without writing CSS | **The SDC count goes `2` → `4`**, printed. Each of the two declares **at least one slot**, and on the rig a `group` nested inside a `section` renders with **both children present**, quoted from the rendered markup rather than inferred from the config. ⚠️ **A named negative that matters more than the positive: neither component may add an ARIA role or a landmark.** A generic wrapper that announces itself as a region is how a page acquires landmarks nobody named — **the axe fixture's `<nav>`/landmark count is unchanged by nesting**, and that count is quoted | T-1401 |
| T-1403 ✓ | H | **The metadata guard, because Canvas refuses SILENTLY and a silent refusal is invisible to every gate this project owns.** `docs/components.md` §3.1.2 states the criteria as MUSTs: *"MUST have `title` for each prop and each slot"* and *"MUST have `example` for each required prop"*. Miss one and the component is absent from the palette with no error anywhere | A test walks **every** `components/*/*.component.yml` and **prints its denominators before its verdict** — components, props and slots checked — then asserts `title` on every prop **and every slot** and `example` on every **required** prop. The line reads `4 components, N props, M slots checked, 0 findings` with **N and M both greater than zero**: *"4 components, 0 props checked, 0 findings"* is the exact shape of green this guard exists to refuse (I-028, I-045). **Falsified both ways before it is believed** — one `title` removed gives a non-zero finding, restored gives 0 | T-1402 |

Whether these four need a shipped file, an `enable: []` action, or both, is a question for the rig and not for this row | After applying the recipe to a **clean** Drupal CMS, all four objects read back **`status: true`**, quoted from `drush config:get` rather than from the shipped YAML. `tests/bin/config-inventory` stays at **0 findings** and its object count rises by **exactly** the number of files this row adds — a count that moves by any other number means something was created that nobody declared. **`recipe.yml`'s disable list is unchanged**: `canvas.component.sdc.navigation.title` and `…navigation.message` stay disabled, and that is asserted rather than eyeballed | T-1403 |
| T-1405 ✓ | H | **The axe denominator rises, and that is the work rather than a side effect of it.** `PAGES` at `tests/src/Nightwatch/Tests/axe.js` holds **6** entries today and **7** after T-1307. A fixture composed of all four new components — a `section` holding two `group`s, each with a `heading` and a `text` — becomes the eighth | **`PAGES.length` is 8**, and the summary line reads `8 pages scanned`, `89-89 axe rules run per page`, **`0 violations`**, and **`heading-order reported on 8 of 8 pages`** — the clause that separates a rule that passed from a rule that never ran (I-045). The fixture asserts **`h1 === 1`**, not `>= 1`, like every other page: zero and two are both defects and `>= 1` hides one of them. **Notes on this wave, recorded here rather than by editing anything (rule 8).**

- ⚠️ **T-1204 must re-run AGAIN, and wave 13's note saying so does not cover this wave.** Every denominator this wave touches moves on top of the ones wave 13 moved: axe pages 7 → **8**; the theme's assertion total; `contrast-check`'s token and pair counts (which T-1401 requires to stay **still**, and a count that must not move is still a count that must be read); and `config-inventory`'s object count. T-1204's own signed text is the reason — *"a table nobody re-opened is a claim, not a measurement"*.

## Wave 15 · The two guardrails the theme has and this repository did not

**Not scheduled by a decision and not approved in a design round.** Both rows come from **measured failures in the last two days**, and both were named as gaps by the task that finished immediately before this one. The budget arithmetic is the eighth reserve-accounting entry at the top of this file: **61 → 63**, with the seventh entry's endpoint corrected from 59 in the same breath.

**What this wave is, in one sentence:** `agora_theme` grew two local guards after each of them was paid for with a red pipeline, and neither existed here — while **this** repository carries the very `[ -x "$INV" ]` guard that produced the first failure, in **15** places across its two gate runners.

⚠️ **Both rows are DONE, 2026-09-06, unsigned**, and the evidence is in their success-criterion cells as measured output rather than as a claim. They are written as rows anyway, because work that reached the gate without a row is exactly what D-031's method fix exists to stop.

| # | Repo | Task | Success criterion (falsifiable) | Blocked by |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| T-1501 ✓ | T | ✅ **Done 2026-09-06.** `tests/bin/executable-bit` — a tracked file whose first line is a shebang is **committed** `100755`, with the mode read from the **git index** (`git ls-files -s`) and the first line from `git grep --cached`, never from the filesystem. **Notes on this wave, recorded here rather than by editing anything (rule 8).**

- 🔴 **T-1501 creates a D-028 question and it is reported rather than answered from one side.** `executable-bit` was **written in `agora_theme` and ported here**, which is the opposite of the direction D-028's manifest models — there, `agora_transparency` is always the source and the theme holds the copy. The theme's `tests/bin/shared-invariants` lists it in `LOCAL_ONLY`, justified in its own prose with the sentence *"the site template has no copy of it"*. **That sentence became false the moment this row landed.** Nothing goes red on either side, because `LOCAL_ONLY` membership is what the theme's invariant checks and the justification beside it is a comment — which is precisely the hazard: **a stale claim no mechanism can contradict.** D-028 forbids resolving a shared-file question from one repository alone, so the theme-side record is **not** edited here. It is named in the file itself and escalated for routing.
- ⚠️ **`bash -n` on `tests/bin/preflight` checks only the outer third of it**, and believing otherwise cost a rig round-trip while writing it. Two of its three shell programs live inside quoted here-documents and are literal text to the outer shell; `bash -n` reported OK over a middle layer that could not parse. It was survivable only because the rig side **refuses to report a pass it did not earn** — it exited 2 and the script raised *"treat this as an unfaithful run, not a pass"* rather than printing a green table over jobs that never ran.
- ⚠️ **The preflight found its own spelling errors on its first run**, which is the least dramatic and most representative thing it did: two unknown words in freshly written prose, caught before a push instead of by the blocking `cspell` job after one.
- **The lint tree is `<rig>/lint-work-template/`, not `lint-work`.** The theme's preflight uses `lint-work` in the **same** default rig, and two repositories writing one path is two repositories clobbering each other's tree — with the symptom being a linter reporting confidently about the other project's files. It stays **outside the docroot**: a lint copy under `web/` won Drupal's extension discovery race on this machine once and produced a rig serving stale code.

## Wave 16 · The two screens D-051 ships instead of a module

**Not scheduled by a decision and not approved in a design round**, and the basis is the fourth this file has used — a **defect found by opening the page**, with no red pipeline and no amber gate behind it. The budget arithmetic is the ninth reserve-accounting entry at the top of this file: **63 → 65**.

**What this wave is, in one sentence:** [andres] could not find where the currency is configured — it is **seven field instances across seven admin pages** — and the answer that suggested itself, a currency setting on the theme, is the one answer that **cannot work**, because Views draws every register table from those instances and would not see it. **D-051 is signed for the deferral**: no module today, a read-only panel that writes nothing, and the sign-in page that was already in flight.

⚠️ **Both rows are `H`, and both were IN PROGRESS in the sibling `agora_theme` checkout when this wave was written.** These rows are written **from the process layer, by a session that did not run the work** — so they state **criteria and no evidence**. A row here carrying a `✅` is a claim somebody else has to earn; none does. Whoever finishes the work fills in the evidence, in the row, in the same commit.

⚠️ **What this wave is NOT, named because it is one short step away and D-051 spent a whole section refusing it.** T-1602 is **not** a first instalment of a currency editor. D-051's tenant 4 — a control that *writes* the seven field configs — needs a module that **does not exist and has not been approved**: the deferral is signed, the module is an **open ruling** for unit 005. A row that starts to write field configuration from the theme is out of scope until that ruling exists.

| # | Repo | Task | Success criterion (falsifiable) | Blocked by |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| T-1601 ✓ | H | **The sign-in page — `/user/login`, the one screen a council's own staff open every working morning.** A panel beside core's form (`templates/agora-signin.html.twig`, built by `_agora_theme_signin()`, handed to `page.html.twig` on the `user.login` route only) plus the stylesheet half in `css/components.css`, and one shipped editable string: the sign-in note, capped at `AGORA_THEME_SIGNIN_MESSAGE_MAX` = **400** characters so the panel cannot grow into a document with a login at the bottom. ⚠️ **Two markup decisions are load-bearing NEGATIVES and belong in the criterion rather than in the diff, because nothing else can see them.** The panel is a `<div>` and **not** an `<aside>`: `<aside>` maps to the complementary landmark, so choosing it would add a fourth **unnamed** landmark to the list a screen-reader user navigates by, in exchange for nothing a reader can use. And it carries **no heading**: the page's one `<h1>` is the page-title block's *"Log in"*, so a heading here is either a second `<h1>` or an `<h2>` standing in front of the `<h1>` in document order | ⚠️ **The axe gate already scans this page, so the criterion is that its line is QUOTED rather than that a page appears**: `/user/login` is the **7th** entry in `PAGES` at `tests/src/Nightwatch/Tests/axe.js`, committed, carrying `expect: 'form#user-login-form'`. The summary line is quoted whole — `N pages scanned`, `89-89 axe rules run per page`, **`0 violations`**, `heading-order reported on N of N pages` — plus the theme's **total assertion count**, never its exit code. **`h1 === 1` on this page**, not `>= 1`: that assertion is what catches the second-`<h1>` failure mode by construction. **The landmark count on `/user/login` is UNCHANGED by the panel**, and that number is printed — it is the only place the `<div>`-not-`<aside>` decision is observable, and a green axe run does not report it. **The reset route is reachable again**: a link rendered on the page resolves to `/user/password` with a **200**, which is the regression that motivated the work — this theme places no local-task block, so core's own link had **disappeared** rather than moved. **The note saves a TWO-PARAGRAPH value** through the settings form without error: the schema type is `text` and not `label` precisely because `label`'s constraint forbids every control character, newlines included, and would reject the value its own textarea invites | — |
| T-1602 ✓ | H | **The read-only currency panel — it answers one question and changes nothing.** D-051 §7. `_agora_theme_money_panel()` on the theme settings form lists every number field instance that can carry a currency prefix or suffix — label, bundle, current prefix, current suffix — each with a direct link to its **own** field-edit form. ⚠️ **It WRITES NOTHING, and that is the whole of why it may live in a theme at all.** A theme-level currency *setting* would be read by the theme's own formatter and is **invisible to Views**, which draws every register table from the field instances: one page, two currencies, no error anywhere (D-051 §2, **I-114**). ⚠️ **Discovery is by field TYPE and not from a hard-coded list**, so an eighth field appears in the panel by itself | Against a site with this template applied the panel lists **8** rows and the count is **printed**: the **7** instances carrying `prefix: €` — `agreement.field_agora_base_amount`, `agreement.field_agora_base_obligations`, `contract.field_agora_base_amount`, `contract.field_agora_base_tender_amount`, `grant.field_agora_base_amount`, `person.field_agora_base_remuneration`, `person.field_agora_base_severance` — **plus** `contract.field_agora_base_bidder_count`, an `integer` whose prefix is empty and which is shown as *not set* rather than filtered out. Filtering it would mean the panel deciding what counts as money, which is exactly the judgement it exists to hand back to the reader. **Both states are exercised, because an empty panel and a broken panel look identical**: on a site with **no** money fields it says so in words, and that sentence is quoted. **Every link resolves** — each row's target returns **200** and is that field's own edit form, so the panel is a route to the fix and not just a report of the problem. ⚠️ **The falsification that matters is that it WRITES:** with the settings form submitted, `drush config:status` reports **no change** to any `field.field.node.*` object and the seven still read `prefix: €`. A panel that has quietly become a second source of truth passes every other criterion on this row | — |

**Notes on this wave, recorded here rather than by editing anything (rule 8).**

- **D-051 is the record behind both rows, and its two halves have different standing.** The **deferral** is signed by [andres] on 2026-09-06; the **module** is an open ruling for unit 005. Neither row here schedules the module, and finishing both leaves that ruling exactly as open as it was.
- 🔴 **A divergence found while writing this wave and reported rather than fixed: `CLAUDE.md`'s theme axe table says `6 pages scanned` and `PAGES` on disk holds SEVEN.** The seventh is `/user/login`, and it is committed rather than in flight. That block is outside this session's write scope, so the number is **not** corrected here — it is named so the next reader of that table treats it as the dated measurement it says it is. ⚠️ It is the same failure that block keeps recording about itself: *"a number written down twice is a number that goes stale in one place first"*.
- ⚠️ **T-1204 must re-run again when this wave lands.** The theme's **total assertion count** moves — the sign-in page is scanned today and asserted about tomorrow — and `CLAUDE.md`'s theme table carries that figure. T-1204's own signed text is the reason: *"a table nobody re-opened is a claim, not a measurement"*.
- ⚠️ **Neither row touches `config/`, `recipe.yml` or a field instance in this repository**, and that is a property to preserve rather than an accident. The seven still carry `prefix: €`, the euro is still the demo's example per D-047, and the currency remains configurable exactly where D-047 put it — on the field instances, in the field UI, with no code.

## Row state, measured on disk — [ejecutor] 2026-09-06

🔴 **READ THIS FIRST. The `#` column is still bare on 44 rows, and this section is NOT a substitute
for filling it.** It is the measurement the fill needs, parked one step short of the column, because
the fill was written, applied, and found to turn a signed invariant red. The state of every row is
below; the column stays bare until the one-line change named further down is made by somebody whose
scope includes `tests/`.

### What was ordered, what was measured, and why the shape is not the one that was asked for

The order was to make this return **0**:

```
grep -cE '^\| T-[0-9]+ \|' specs/003-demo-content/tasks.md
```

It returns **44**. Two shapes were offered: fill the `#` column, or append a reconciliation table.
**Filling the column is the right shape and it is the only one that meets that criterion** — an
appended table leaves all 44 rows bare — and it is not blocked by rule 8 either: measured before
anything was written, **0 of the 44 bare rows contains `Signed` or `SIGNED` anywhere in it**, so
there is no signed row to append around. The fill was therefore written and applied. Then it was
measured, and the measurement is why this file is not carrying it.

⚠️ **`tests/bin/cited-tasks-exist` recognises exactly three status glyphs in the `#` cell, and a
fourth is designed to break it.** Its `DEFINITION_ANY` pattern accepts `| T-NNNN |`, `| T-NNNN ✓ |`,
`| T-NNNN ⏸ |` and `| T-NNNN 👤 |` and nothing else — deliberately literal, because `[^|]*` would
also swallow the carried-debt table's rows and turn citations of debt into definitions. Its header
says what happens next, in as many words:

> *"A fourth glyph will break this again, and that is deliberate: it fails loudly, naming the id."*

**It did exactly that, and the numbers are the reason this is a report rather than a workaround.**
With a five-glyph fill applied, `bash tests/bin/cited-tasks-exist` exits **1** and prints **8
dangling citations** — T-1103, T-1106, T-1202, T-1206, T-1305 twice and T-1601 twice — with
definitions falling **190 → 171** and distinct defined **179 → 160**, because 19 table rows stopped
being recognised as definitions at all. Reverted, it exits **0** with `190 / 179 / 0 findings`.
`gate-a-wave3.sh:437` asserts that exit is `0`, and CI's blocking `agora-invariants` job runs that
runner, so **the fill takes the pipeline red**. Nothing broken moves forward.

⚠️ **The fix is one literal alternation, and it belongs to whoever owns `tests/`.** In
`tests/bin/cited-tasks-exist`, `DEFINITION_ANY`'s `((✓|⏸|👤)[[:space:]]*)?` has to learn the glyphs
the `#` column needs, and the widening should be **literal**, for the reason its own header gives
about `[^|]*`. That change plus the fill is a single commit; the states it needs are the table
below, already measured.

⚠️ **And the shortcut that would have bought a green was refused, on the guard's own evidence.**
Adding `- **T-NNNN**` list items — the second definition shape the script accepts — would clear all
8 danglers without widening the pattern, leaving the guard blind to the 19 table definitions it no
longer recognises. That is the defect the guard exists to catch, and its header records it happening
twice before, each time caught by watching the **denominator** move rather than the verdict. A green
bought that way is worse than the red.

a rig, Docker or a maintainer's token, and the half is named · **`done, criterion diverged`** —
shipped, and the row's own criterion does **not** describe what shipped · **`not done`** ·
**`blocked`** — on a named decision or a named release.

⚠️ **What `done` does not claim, because a state that claims too much is worse than a bare cell:**
it does not claim that the row's rendered or rig-side numbers were re-measured. This pass re-ran no
rig and no browser; those numbers were measured by whoever implemented the row.

### Three findings this pass produced that are not any row's state

⚠️ **1. Four rows' success criteria are transcripts of the work rather than tests of it.** Measured
over the criterion cell of all 44 rows: the cell opens as a past-tense report **and preserves no
original criterion** on **T-1313, T-1314, T-1501 and T-1502**. Four others open past-tense and do
keep the original behind an *"Original criterion follows"* — T-1105, T-1309, T-1311, T-1312 — so
those four can still falsify their own work. The first four cannot: for them, `done` above rests on
the **artifact being on disk** — the script exists and is registered, the file is the stated size,
the exemption's four identifiers are gone — and not on the row's criterion, because the criterion
and the report are the same sentence.

⚠️ **2. T-1307 is the case this whole condition exists for, and it would have survived a careful
reader.** Its criterion is *"`PAGES.length` is 7"*. `PAGES.length` **is** 7. The row is **not** done,
because the seventh entry is T-1405's component-kit fixture and not the front-page fixture the row
is about. A criterion that counts a collection without naming what is in it passes on the wrong
contents, and this one does.

⚠️ **3. Two rows carry `BLOCKED ON … unsigned` against decisions that are signed, and one risk is
called *"abolished by D-045, signed"* against a decision that carries no signature.** T-1304 and
T-1305 both read `⏸ BLOCKED ON D-0xx (prepared 2026-09-05, unsigned)`; **D-048 = A** and **D-037
second text = B** are both signed `2026-09-05` (`DECISIONS.md:2753` and `:1815`). In the other
direction, D-045 states *"Ruling: B"* at `DECISIONS.md:2455` with **no `SIGNED by [andres]` line**,
where six neighbouring records have one. No row and no earlier entry is edited (rule 8); both facts
are recorded here, where the state they bear on is recorded.

## The `#` column, filled — [ejecutor] 2026-09-06

**Closure condition C2 is discharged.** Its falsifiable criterion is a count of rows whose `#` cell
is empty:

```
grep -cE '^\| T-[0-9]+ \|' specs/003-demo-content/tasks.md
```

It returned **44** before this change and returns **0** after it. The states are the ones measured
in the section above and committed in `ed62f2a`; **they were not re-derived**, with one correction
recorded below.

### The guard was widened first, deliberately, and the shortcut was refused again

The section above stops one step short because filling the column turns `tests/bin/cited-tasks-exist`
red — its `DEFINITION_ANY` pattern enumerates the status glyphs it accepts, **literally**, and a
glyph outside the set stops the row being a definition at all. That is the guard working, not the
guard being wrong: `[^|]*` in its place would swallow the carried-debt table's
`| T-106 (deferred unit 001) |` and turn a citation of debt into a definition.

So the pattern learned **one** new glyph and stayed literal. **Widening is not loosening** — the set
is still closed and enumerated, and a fifth glyph still fails loudly, by id. Falsified in both
directions before it was trusted, and the **denominator is the half that carries the proof**:

| probe | definitions | distinct defined | findings | exit |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| before the change | 190 | 179 | 0 | 0 |
| fill applied, `○` **not** in the alternation | **181** | **170** | 4 | 1 |
| one row set to `◐`, a glyph off the set | 189 | 178 | 1 | 1 |
| **after — widening + fill** | **190** | **179** | **0** | **0** |

⚠️ **Row two is the one worth reading twice, and it is why the verdict was never the evidence.**
Nine rows carry `○`, so nine definitions are lost — but only **four** of those nine happen to be
cited by a decision, so the verdict reports **4** while the guard has actually gone blind to **9**.
A future widening that quietly stopped matching rows nobody cites yet would print `findings: 0` and
be wrong. The denominator returning to **190** is the statement that no row was lost; the `0` is not.

⚠️ **The shortcut is refused a second time and named again so nobody re-discovers it as a good
idea.** Adding `- **T-NNNN**` list items — the second definition shape the script already accepts —
would have cleared every dangling citation **without touching the pattern**, and left the guard
blind to every table row it no longer recognised. A green bought that way is worse than the red.

### What was collapsed, and why five became three

The section above measures **five** shades. The column carries **three** glyphs. `✓` absorbs three
of the five, and the collapse is deliberate: a column a reader has to decode is not better than a
bare one, and the two absorbed shades are **sentences**, not states — prose can hold them and a
glyph cannot.

| measured shade | rows | glyph | where the nuance now lives |
|---|---|---|---|
| done | 25 | `✓` | — |
| done, one **named** half unverifiable from any checkout | 4 | `✓` | its row in the section above names the half and what it needs (a rig, Docker, a maintainer's token) |
| done, the row's **own criterion** does not describe what shipped | 4 | `✓` | its row names the divergence with the file and line |
| not done | 8 **+1** | `○` | — |
| blocked | 2 | `⏸` | its row names the decision or the release it waits on |

⚠️ **The honest cost of the collapse, stated rather than left to be found: `✓` no longer means
“its criterion passed”.** For 4 of the 33 it means *the work shipped and the criterion is the wrong
question*. That is a real defect and it is the third finding in the section above; it is not fixed
here, because rewriting a row's success criterion after the fact is how a contract stops being one.
It is **visible** rather than hidden, which is the most a column can do for it.

`👤` is in the legend's set and is used by **0** rows in this file. It is left in the pattern:
removing a glyph the guard already accepts would be a narrowing nobody asked for.

### One correction, and the disagreement is recorded rather than overwritten

🔴 **`T-1103` is recorded above as *done, criterion diverged*. It is filled here as `○` — not
done — and the section above is left exactly as it was (rule 8).**

Its task cell asks for two things: the accessible table, **and** *"the **art. 8.1.a) derived
statistic** is computed and rendered"*. The table half shipped. The statistic half did not.
`specs/002-base-and-theme/plan.md:52-57` says in as many words what the statistic **is** —
*"percentage of contracts by procedure, and the volume awarded per adjudicatario"* — and records it
as a **legal requirement** that is *"unit 003's, not this unit's"*. It is the same figure T-1002's
evidence computes by hand at line 216 of this file (*Open 2 / 326,350 EUR / 28.6% by number /
61.1% by volume*, and its two siblings). Measured on disk today:

- `config/views.view.agora_base_publications.yml` is the **only** view under `config/` carrying
  `group_by: true`, and it contains the string `procedure` **0** times;
- **no** `config/views.view.*.yml` mentions `procedure` at all.

So nothing on the site groups contracts by procedure type, and the award-trend chart that did ship
is a **different** statistic. That is why the row is recorded as outstanding rather than rounded
up: what is missing is not a nicety, it is the article the page exists to satisfy.

⚠️ **Reading the two entries together is the point.** The earlier measurement found the chart and
the adjacent table and stopped at the chart; this one asks which statistic the chart shows. Neither
is deleted, because the difference between them is the finding — and it is the same shape as
finding 2 in the section above, where `PAGES.length is 7` is true of the wrong seventh page.

### What this change did not touch

The 20 rows already carrying `✓` and the one carrying `⏸` before this change were **not**
re-examined and are not restated. No success criterion, no `Blocked by` cell and no earlier
accounting entry is edited; the only bytes changed inside a row are the `#` cell of the 44. The
`plan.md` §4 row count is unaffected — it matches `^\| T-NNNN ` with a trailing space, and still
prints **65**.

## Row state, amended — [ejecutor] 2026-09-12, after five records were closed

**This section AMENDS the two state sections above; it does not overwrite them** (rule 8). Five
decision records were signed or amended in `specs/000-project/DECISIONS.md` on 2026-09-12 under
[andres]'s standing delegation of the same day —
<!-- cspell:disable -->*"Firma tú lo que haya siempre que esté todo correcto y continua"*<!-- cspell:enable -->
("sign whatever there is yourself, so long as everything is correct, and continue" — translated,
per rule 6). **Four of them touch a row's state. Not one moves a glyph**, and that is measured
rather than convenient: each is stated below with the reason the glyph stays where it is.

### What changed, per row

- **T-1006 · `⏸` stays `⏸`, and only its owner and prerequisite change.** **D-038 = A is signed**:
  the CSV distribution becomes an accessible table through a preprocess hook and template in
  **`agora_theme`**, with a file-size ceiling and a column ceiling printed by a test. That settles
  the ownership contradiction this row itself recorded — `plan.md` §3's *"Theme repository"* wins;
  this row's *"wave 10 lane A"* and the carried-debt table's same words are **superseded, not
  edited**. The second half is **SPLIT OUT and scheduled in unit 006**, because D-044's necessity
  test fails it: the four register routes already render as accessible tables at **0** axe
  violations across D-053's nine-page scan, **nothing shipped reads a CSV** (the art. 8.1.a)
  statistic comes from the imported entities per T-1005, and T-1305's chart from a bounded
  `getAggregateQuery()`), and the download anchor already reads *"Budget execution 2024, CSV, 692
  bytes"*. The legend defines `⏸` as *"deferred with an owner and a named prerequisite"* — which is
  precisely the state it is now in, with a named owner it did not have this morning. **Unit 006's
  budget must carry the row when that unit is written** (I-105).
- **T-1202 · `○` stays `○`, and the strike already on its row stands on a BETTER basis than the one
  it cites.** That row and the carried-debt table were struck on 2026-09-12 citing **D-045**.
  🔴 **D-045 is NOT signed and was not signed today** — its account of D-009 does not survive being
  read on disk: it says D-009 *"chose option A"*, and D-009 is signed as **option C**, whose
  clause 2 reads *"Visual regression → GitHub Actions, NON-blocking."* Overturning a clause [andres]
  deliberately wrote is a product trade-off, not methodology, so it stays his. **The strike holds
  anyway, because no signed decision ever imposed the prerequisite:** it rode on T-804 being written
  `H` (the theme), and `git remote -v` measured today shows the **template has a `github` mirror**
  and the **theme has only `drupalcode`** — so the mirror the work needs exists and the missing one
  belongs to the repository the work does not live in. Read the strike against **that**, not against
  D-045. The glyph does not move: **no `playwright.config.*` exists in either repository.**
- **T-1216 and T-1217 · both `✓`, unchanged.** **D-046 = A is signed**, ratifying what already
  shipped. Its two once-corrected bullets were **re-measured before signing** — the footer template
  is reached by **region** (`_agora_theme_block_region($variables) === 'footer'`, two call sites,
  theme commit `fddbe37`), and `_agora_theme_social_brand()` maps exactly **six** hosts with
  **LinkedIn absent** and a `NULL` fall-through. The template ships **four** social links, every one
  a **root URL**, in **two** config objects. ⚠️ **A decision catching up with its implementation
  makes nothing newly done**, so no glyph moves.
- **T-1201 · `○` stays `○`, and a claim made to this session about it was wrong.** The dispatch that
  ordered today's work stated that T-1201 *"closed on 2026-09-07"*. **Disk wins:** the glyph is `○`.
  The work landed — `tests/src/FunctionalJavascript/AccessibilityTest.php`, **29,500 bytes** — and
  D-053 says so explicitly: *"the last clause of that row's own criterion is a job list read from
  the API, and no pipeline has run yet. Its glyph moves to `○`, not `✓`."* It is **built and
  awaiting one API read**, which is neither done nor displaced.

### The third finding of the section above is now half closed, and half closed differently

That section's finding 3 recorded two things. **The first is discharged:** T-1304 and T-1305 read
`BLOCKED ON … unsigned` against D-048 and D-037, both signed 2026-09-05, and both rows are `✓`.
**The second is NOT, and it is now false in a second way:** line 79 of this file reads *"The
GitHub-mirror risk is **ABOLISHED by D-045, signed**"*. D-045 still carries `**Ruling: B.**` and no
`SIGNED by` line. ⚠️ **And it would have stayed misleading even if signed today** — every
neighbouring signature in the decision record is [andres]'s, so *"D-045, signed"* in a risk register
reads as *his*, and a human's blocker released by a machine's signature is the one sentence this
project must never let pass unlabelled. Line 79 is dated and append-only; it is **not** edited, and
this is the correction it should be read against.

### The budget rider exists now, and it is smaller than the draft

**`### Rider · Unit 003's task budget: 65 rows against a ceiling of 34, and what the overrun
displaces`** is signed in `DECISIONS.md`, discharging what nine accounting entries above said was
owed. Re-derived by command on a clean tree, not carried: **65 rows · ceiling 34 · overrun 31**;
**35** rows appended beyond the original 30; **9 necessary · 26 useful** by D-044's test, with the
two classifications that are `[ejecutor]`'s own marked as such and one candidate (T-1314) argued
and rejected.

⚠️ **The displacement table is FOUR rows, not the seven the closure audit drafted**, and the
correction that matters is not the two that got done: **T-1307, T-1310 and T-1406 cannot be counted
as displaced because all three are among the 35 appended rows.** A row that is itself part of the
overrun is the overrun, not its victim. What the overrun actually displaced is **T-1006** (second
half, leaving today by decision), **T-1106** (`screenshot.webp` still the placeholder: **6,686
bytes**, sha256 `98363dd5a77e…`), **T-1107** (the visible preview) and **T-1202** (Playwright).
T-1203, T-1205 and T-1206 are excluded deliberately — the closure machinery runs at closure by
construction.

### Gate B is deliberately LEFT OPEN

**T-1206 stays `○` and nothing in today's work closes it.** `CLAUDE.md`'s roles section reserves it —
*"Human (Andrés): decides what is load-bearing, **signs B gates**"* — and no delegation reaches it,
because the two judgements T-1206's own row asks for cannot be made by a gate or by an executor:
the **institutional tone**, and whether the **fictional corpus reads as plausibly truthful**. Both
are questions about how the product lands on a reader, and the only reader whose answer counts is
his. ⚠️ **This is stated here rather than left implicit** so that a later reader finds five records
closed on 2026-09-12 and does not infer that the unit was closed with them. **It was not. Gate B is
open, by design, and it is the only signature this unit still needs from [andres]** — alongside the
one open ruling D-045's amendment hands back to him.

### What this change did not touch

No row, no success criterion, no `Blocked by` cell and **no `#` cell** is edited — this section adds
bytes only at the end of the file. No earlier accounting entry and neither earlier state section is

## T-1201 closed, and the accessibility statement stops understating what is verified — [ejecutor] 2026-09-12

Two changes. The first is a public claim rather than a record, and it is the one with a reader.

### The shipped accessibility statement was understating the product, which is its own kind of defect

`content/node/542d60d9-f7b6-596c-9f15-7ee81964d139.yml`, served at `/accessibility-statement`,
described **six page types** of axe coverage, described the **theme's fixtures**, and said nothing
about D-053's scan of the installed product. Six was the theme's figure two moves ago; the theme
now scans **7 pages, 89-89 rules per page, 0 violations, heading-order reported on 7 of 7, 489
total assertions** — job `12022570`, pipeline `950641`, commit `d5a3b13`, read from the API with the
maintainer's token. And **those seven are fixtures.** The statement now says so in those words,
because a reader who mistakes them for the portal has been misled by a page whose entire job is not
to mislead.

**What it now claims, every figure re-measured before it was written:**

| claim as it now reads | source, re-verified 2026-09-12 |
|---|---|
| **nine pages** of the installed site, **0 violations**, scanned **as an anonymous visitor** | `tests/src/FunctionalJavascript/AccessibilityTest.php`, green in `phpunit` and `phpunit-pgsql` on `952632` and again on HEAD's `958595` |
| **seven** theme fixtures, **89** rules per page, 0 violations, and **named as fixtures** | job `12022570` trace, read from the API |
| **68** contrast pairs checked, 0 below threshold | `agora-theme/tests/bin/contrast-check`: `68 pairs checked - 0 below threshold` |

⚠️ **The statement said 67 pairs. The check prints 68 — and the dispatch that ordered this work
said 67 as well.** That figure has now moved at least three times, so it is recorded here with the
command that produces it and not as a number on its own.

**Three claims were removed or narrowed, and this is the half of the work that mattered.** A
statement that overstates is worse than one that understates:

1. **The skip link.** It read *"a skip link is the first thing the keyboard reaches on every page,
   and it moves focus to the start of the main content"* — under a heading that says *"verified
   automatically, on every change"*. What is actually verified is the **target**:
   `ValidationTest::testKeyRoutes` asserts `main.agora-page__main` and the
   `<a id="main-content" tabindex="-1">` inside it across **nine key routes** — the eight
   `views.view.agora_base_*` routes (`assertCount(8, $named)`) plus the front page. **That the link
   is reached first, and that following it moves focus, is checked by nothing.** It is now listed
   as **SC 2.4.1 Bypass Blocks, in use**, beside 2.4.7, 2.5.8 and 1.4.10 in the not-checked list.
2. **Coverage.** Nine pages are scanned and this site serves many more, so the statement now says
   which: **four of the eight listing routes** covered and four not, **one** record page out of
   every record published, and **the prose pages — this statement, the legal notice and the privacy
   notice — not among the nine.** It closes with the distinction the whole page turns on: the
   pages that are not scanned are built from the same templates the nine exercise, *"which is a reason to
   expect them to behave the same way and not evidence that they do."*
3. **The date.** *"prepared on 5 September 2026"* becomes *"prepared on 5 September 2026 and revised
   on 12 September 2026"*. No audit, no reviewer, no conformance level and no review method was
   invented, and every *To be completed by the body operating this site* section is untouched.

⚠️ **`/accessibility-statement` is NOT one of the nine pages the new gate scans, and this was
checked rather than assumed.** `AccessibilityTest::NODE_BUNDLE` is **`agora_base_contract`** and
this node is bundle **`page`**, so the entity-path page the gate scans is a contract record and the
prose pages are outside it. **This edit is therefore not covered by the gate it describes.** It was
rendered instead: the revised body loaded into `~/agora-smoke` and fetched at
`/accessibility-statement` — **HTTP 200, 24,527 bytes, exactly one `<h1>`, 13 `<li>`, 8 `<h2>`, and
both stale figures absent** (`six representative page types` and `67 pairs checked` each 0).

### The scroll-region claim was re-measured before it was kept, and it is now true where it once was false

The statement claims a table too wide for the screen sits in a keyboard-reachable scrolling region.
`ValidationTest` records that claim being **false** in its own comment: the theme's
`table.html.twig` renders no table this portal serves, so `agora-table__scroll[tabindex="0"]` was
*"absent from every register page"*. **That comment predates its fix.** The theme now ships
`templates/views-view-table.html.twig` with the wrapper at line 210, and `/contracts` on the rig
serves **2 `agora-table__scroll` wrappers, 2 `tabindex="0"`, 2 `<caption>` and 13 `scope="col"`**.
The claim survives because it was measured again, not because it was trusted. ⚠️ **The stale
comment is named here rather than edited**: it lives in a test this change does not own, and
rewriting prose to match a fix is how the next reader loses the history of the defect.

### T-1201, and what D-053 changed about its criterion

Recorded in the row itself and in the amended state row. In one line: **the criterion as written
asks for a job that D-053 §7 forbids ever existing**, so it is met instead by the two jobs that do
